# PROCEEDINGS COMMENCED BY CERTIFICATE OF OFFENCE

> **Unofficial version.** Text obtained from the official Ontario website via the [A2AJ Canadian Laws dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/a2aj/canadian-laws). Always check the [official source](https://www.ontario.ca/laws/api/v2/legislation/en/doc-search/regulation/900950) before relying on it.

R.R.O. 1990, Reg. 950

Note: This regulation is not fully bilingual as many of its Schedules are in English only.

1.-4.3.1 Revoked: O. Reg. 106/11, s. 1.

4.4 The clerk of the court or an assistant clerk, if so authorized by the clerk, is a designated person for the purposes of subsections 69 (3) and (5) of the Act.

4.5 Revoked: O. Reg. 68/12, s. 1.

5 (1) The words or expressions set out in Column 2 of a Schedule may be used in a certificate of offence, offence notice or summons to designate the offence described in the provision set out opposite in Column 3 of the Schedule under the Act or regulation set out in the heading to the Schedule.

(2) For the purposes of subsection (1), either the English version or the French version, if any, of a Schedule may be used.

6 Revoked: O. Reg. 192/24, s. 1.

7 Revoked: O. Reg. 682/92, s. 1.

### Forms 1-9 Revoked: O. Reg. 106/11, s. 2.
